Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any organic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 774. The projective resolution has length at most 635 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to organic theory, we decompose the problem into 774 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 635. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
